| 2026-11-11 17:30 UTC | 2026-11-11 19:30 UTC | Basketball - Eurocup: Siauliai - Tenerife | Eurocup was created in 2002 as the second competition of Euroleague Basketball. The strongest basketball clubs that fail to qualify for the Euroleague compete in this competition, and mostly clubs from the top 15 strongest European leagues participate in it. Placement in the Eurocup is secured through national championships and cups, and through invitations to the so-called wildcards. The second strongest competition in European basketball was created by the merger of the FIBA ​​Radivoje Korać Cup and the Saporta Cup. The significance of this competition is that the winner secures the right to participate in the next season in the strongest club basketball competition in Europe, the Euroleague. |

The strongest basketball clubs that fail to qualify for the Euroleague compete in this competition, and mostly clubs from the top 15 strongest European leagues participate in it. Placement in the Eurocup is secured through national championships and cups, and through invitations to the so-called wildcards. The second strongest competition in European basketball was created by the merger of the FIBA ​​Radivoje Korać Cup and the Saporta Cup. The significance of this competition is that the winner secures the right to participate in the next season in the strongest club basketball competition in Europe, the Euroleague. |

| 2026-11-13 18:30 UTC | 2026-11-13 20:30 UTC | Košarka - ABA liga: Cibona - FMP | The ABA league was founded by the best basketball clubs from Bosnia and Herzegovina, Montenegro, Croatia, Serbia and Slovenia. As such, it consists of clubs from the territory of the former SFRY, and representatives of Hungary, Bulgaria, Israel and the Czech Republic also participated in it. It changed its name on several occasions depending on the sponsor, and today its official name is the Adriatic Basketball Association (ABA) League. The competition is played in two parts, the first part takes place with all teams playing two games, each against each other, one at home and one away, i.e. according to the two-round league system. The second part of the competition, i.e. the playoffs, differed from season to season. |

Kao vrhunac 3 na 3 košarke World Tour natjecanje pod okriljem Fibe od 2012. godine mami uzdahe ljubitelja ovog dinamičnog sporta. Temeljna razlika između klasične dvoranske košarke je broj igrača na terenu, a to je po tri u svakoj momčadi. Bitne razlike su još, da se igra na polovici standardnog košarkaškog terena na jedan koš. | 2026-11-11 01:35 UTC | 2026-11-11 02:41 UTC | Giroud: The 40th birthday dinner | Quoi de mieux que d'être entouré de ses proches pour passer le cap de la quarantaine ? Canal+ a décidé de célébrer l'anniversaire d'Olivier Giroud en réunissant autour de lui ses amis. Dans un restaurant lillois, l'attaquant champion du monde a dîné en compagnie des consultants Christophe Jallet et Robert Pirès, mais également de son ami, l'ancien international Mathieu Debuchy. Pendant plus d'une heure, l'ancien buteur des Bleus se livre comme rarement : sa future reconversion, les critiques sur son parcours, les transferts manqués, les treize ans à l'étranger, l'armoire à trophées. Sans oublier des messages d'anniversaire surprises d'anciens coéquipiers, des anecdotes décalées sur sa riche carrière et les bougies soufflées ! |

The moment is brief, unique. The moment and the gesture are suspended, in time and in the air. Amara Simba, Jean-Pierre Papin, Olivier Giroud, Djibril Cissé, Lilian Laslandes, Eugénie Le Sommer, Camille Abily tell their lives as “Football Acrobats”. |

How could athletes who grew up in an ultra-privileged world develop such a rage to win? The “Made in Monaco” athletes tell their stories, explain what pushes them to surpass themselves, and share the major rules of life that they have given themselves to reach the summits. |

Après une première tentative stoppée à 50 mètres de l'arrivée, en hypothermie et à la limite de la noyade, la Normande de 27 ans part retenter sa chance quatre ans plus tard, malgré le traumatisme. | 2026-11-13 20:02 UTC | 2026-11-13 21:17 UTC | Little Big Fred | Il a passé sa vie entière sous les projecteurs, avant d'être aspiré par ses ténèbres intérieures. Désormais consultant, Fred Weis a été un joueur de basket au talent précoce et un précurseur. Du haut de ses 2,17m, le pivot a porté l'équipe de France jusqu'à la finale des Jeux olympiques 2000 face aux Etats-Unis et a prouvé que la fluidité et l'intelligence de jeu n'étaient pas réservées aux petits gabarits. Courtisé par les plus grands clubs, en Europe comme aux Etats-Unis, il renonce finalement à faire le grand saut vers la NBA préférant revenir soutenir son club de coeur, Limoges. Sa carrière exemplaire est subitement chamboulée par la découverte de l'autisme de son fils, un choc qui l'entraine dans une spirale autodestructrice, jusqu'à envisager les solutions les plus radicales. |
